Campaign, seed 928, difficulty Captain, permadeath on, scheme TwoRollAverage, 10 maps
-- The Field Before the Keep --
Hollin is a sworn village, a day short of the keep. Nobody in it is
hungry. Nobody is fighting. Every hearth is lit, which is more than
Ironwake can say. Nobody will meet my eye.

I asked a girl at the well her name. She said, "We're well, thank you,"
and went back to the bucket.

Past Hollin the keep is in sight across a field and a river. The rime on
its walls has started to run.

(Placeholder card until the writing pass. Defeat the Sworn Captain. Each
group sleeps until a fight comes within its hearing.)

-- Before map 9 of 10: The Field Before the Keep; the purse holds 500 --
== Roster ==
  Alder Fenn (captain): Cadet L5, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3; promotes at 3: Marshal, Ranger or Vanguard
  Wren: Cadet L5, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Teodor: Pikeman L5, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3, 3: Family Lance 40/40
  Ottilie: Bowman L5, EXP 0; 1: Iron Bow 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Pell: Adept L5, EXP 0; 1: Cinder 8/8, 2: Gust 6/6
  Dunstan: Bulwark L5, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40
  Maud: Chaplain L5, EXP 0; 1: Radiance 5/5, 2: Salve 8/8
  Keziah: Reaver L5, EXP 0; 1: Kinsbane 20/20, 2: Iron Axe 40/40, 3: Iron Gauntlets 40/40
  Brannock: Cadet L5, EXP 0; 1: Hatchet 45/45, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
Deploys to The Field Before the Keep: Alder Fenn, Teodor, Ottilie, Pell, Maud, Keziah (deploy 6 of 9)
Rook (Skyrider, level 5) rides with the enemy on this map.
Keziah's talk turns her; the captain's spares her (talk <unit> rook). Turned, she joins only if a bed is free (beds: 9/12), and a death never frees one.
Met on the road: Ansgar (Outrider, level 5) (meet <unit>; one, final).
Ansgar joins only if a bed is free (beds: 9/12), and a death never frees one. Not met, he goes on alone.
== Keep ==
Purse: 500
Rooms: beds: 9/12; a fallen member keeps their bed
  bunk: Bunk room, 400, +2 beds, built 0 of 2
  forge: Forge, 600, Refine +5 acc or +1 power a step, built 0 of 1
  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 3 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; needs the Barracks first
  Stores: common 0, frozen iron 0; a step costs one and 100, shop weapons to +2 on common, the main line's signatures to +3 on frozen iron
Keep: Ironwake Keep; built: nothing; the purse holds 500
  wall: Rebuild a wall, 400
    Wall 10,3: Wall; no unit can stand on it; the gap 10,3 to 10,4 narrows from 2 tiles to 1 (10,4)
    Wall 10,8: Wall; no unit can stand on it; the gap 10,7 to 10,8 narrows from 2 tiles to 1 (10,7)
  ditch: Dig a ditch, 300
    Ditch 9,5: Water; infantry, cavalry and armored units cannot stand on it; flying can
    Ditch 9,6: Water; infantry, cavalry and armored units cannot stand on it; flying can
== Quests ==
Side maps (the member and one ally you pick, not the captain; a lost side map never ends the campaign):
  maud_1: Maud's quest 1, The Lazar House (quest maud_1 <ally>); permadeath applies
  pell_1: Pell's quest 1, The Burned School (quest pell_1 <ally>); permadeath applies
== Shop ==

Trials in place of a seal (one attempt per unit and class before each map): Bulwark, Outrider
